  <!-- ===== 情绪波浪 ===== -->
  <section class="tab on" id="tab-wave">
    <div class="alert-banner" id="alertBanner"><span class="warn">⚠</span>如果没有让你感到兴奋的机会，就果断选择空仓！</div>
    <div class="pos-banner" id="posBanner">
      <div class="pos-head">
        <span class="pos-title">次日仓位上限 · 动态管理（按情绪阶段）</span>
        <span class="pos-zone" id="posZone">—</span>
      </div>
      <div class="pos-track"><div class="pos-fill" id="posFill"></div></div>
      <div class="pos-meta" id="posMeta"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="cards" id="waveCards"></div>
    <div class="toolbar" id="waveTb">
      <button data-n="10">近10日</button>
      <button data-n="30" class="on">近30日</button>
      <button data-n="60">近60日</button>
      <button data-n="120">近120日</button>
      <button data-n="0">全部</button>
    </div>
    <div class="panel"><h2>跌超7% 恐慌指标（对数轴 · 底10）</h2><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="cPanic"></canvas></div>
      <div class="note">单日跌幅&gt;7%的个股数量，采用对数轴（底10）：极端行情不再把正常日压扁，冰点日与常态日都清晰可辨（数值下限取1以保证对数可见）。</div></div>
    <div class="panel"><h2>炸板率</h2><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="cZb"></canvas></div>
      <div class="note">炸板率%=炸板数 /(涨停封板数+炸板数)。<b>多数时间处于 17%~30% 正常态</b>，无需过度解读。关键判读：① 炸板率高（&gt;30%）但跌停板/跌超7%数量稀少、环境向好 → 多为<b>趋势行情</b>（大盘单边向上），连板赚钱效应弱，<b>应回避连板</b>；② 高炸板率伴随跌停/跌超7%同步放大 → 才是真退潮风险。非趋势情境下，高炸板率多为冰点反转前的分歧/洗盘，次日常均值回归反弹，并非风险信号（前瞻风险在「过热侧」，见溢价图过热线）。</div></div>
    <div class="panel"><h2>涨停家数 vs 跌停家数（跌停独立右轴）</h2><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="cMain"></canvas></div>
      <div class="note">红=涨停（左轴），绿=跌停（右轴，独立刻度，不再被涨停压制）。两者剪刀差即市场强弱：跌停从个位数爬升即预警，百股跌停为极端冰点。<b>虚线为警戒线：涨停≥80家、跌停≥5家</b>——任一被触及即提示情绪过热/风险。</div></div>
    <div class="panel"><h2>连板高度（最高连板数 · 左10cm / 右20cm）</h2><div id="boardKpi" class="cards" style="margin-bottom:12px"></div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="cBoard"></canvas></div>
      <div class="note">纵轴为当日市场最高连板数（取 zt_pool 中该日真实最大连板数；代码 30/688 开头计为 20cm，其余计 10cm）；每根日柱左红=10cm 最高连板、右蓝=20cm 最高连板。有 zt_pool 数据的日期为真实值，历史日（数据库尚无个股级数据）回退情绪分布图估算并标「板+（情绪图估算）」。柱越高=连板空间越大。</div></div>
    <div class="panel"><h2>昨日涨停 / 昨日连板 溢价（次日表现）</h2><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="cPrem"></canvas></div>
      <div class="note">红线=昨日涨停股今日平均溢价，蓝线=昨日连板股今日平均溢价（均由「昨日涨停股池」计算，无需爬虫）。<b>虚线=2% 警戒线</b>：低于此值亏钱效应放大、情绪转弱；0% 以下即整体亏钱。<b>红色虚线=过热风险前瞻线(p90)</b>：溢价越过此线即「高潮」，次日均值回归回落概率高，应主动收手（对应仓位上限→空仓）。数据随每日运行 main.py 自动累积。</div></div>
  </section>

<script>
/* ---------- Tab 切换 ---------- */
document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(b=>{
  b.onclick=()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
    b.classList.add('on');
    document.getElementById('tab-'+b.dataset.tab).classList.add('on');
    if(window.__renderTab) window.__renderTab(b.dataset.tab);
  };
});
/* ---------- Chart.js 按需懒加载（避免阻塞首屏解析） ---------- */
let __chartP=null;
function ensureChart(){
  if(window.Chart) return Promise.resolve(window.Chart);
  if(__chartP) return __chartP;
  __chartP=new Promise((res,rej)=>{
    const s=document.createElement('script');
    s.src='https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.3/dist/chart.umd.min.js';
    s.onload=()=>res(window.Chart);
    s.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('Chart.js 加载失败，请检查网络'));
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  });
  return __chartP;
}
/* ---------- tab 懒渲染：默认激活的 tab 首屏渲染，其余点击时才渲染 ---------- */
window.__tabRenders=window.__tabRenders||{};
window.__tabDone=window.__tabDone||{};
window.__renderTab=function(tab){
  if(window.__tabDone[tab]) return;
  const fn=window.__tabRenders[tab];
  if(fn){ window.__tabDone[tab]=true; Promise.resolve().then(fn); }
};
window.__registerTab=function(tab, fn){
  window.__tabRenders[tab]=fn;
  const btn=document.querySelector('nav button[data-tab="'+tab+'"]');
  if(btn && btn.classList.contains('on') && !window.__tabDone[tab]){
    window.__tabDone[tab]=true; Promise.resolve().then(fn);
  }
};
/* ---------- 子标签切换（板块题材内：行业资金流 / 概念板块） ---------- */
document.querySelectorAll('.sub-nav button').forEach(b=>{
  b.onclick=()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.sub-nav button').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.sub-tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
    b.classList.add('on');
    document.getElementById('sub-'+b.dataset.sub).classList.add('on');
    // 触发子标签对应的懒渲染（与主导航 __registerTab 机制对齐）
    const sub = b.dataset.sub;
    if(window.__tabRenders[sub] && !window.__tabDone[sub]){
      window.__tabDone[sub]=true;
      Promise.resolve().then(window.__tabRenders[sub]);
    }
  };
});

/* ---------- 通用图表样式 ---------- */

  return Object.assign({
    responsive:true, maintainAspectRatio:false,
    interaction:{mode:'index', intersect:false},
    plugins:{ legend:{labels:{boxWidth:12,font:{size:11},color:'#555'}} },
    scales:{
      x:{ ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}}, grid:{display:false} },
      y:{ ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10}}, grid:{color:GRID} }
    }
  }, extra||{});
}

  // ---- 次日最大仓位上限（由 build 注入 const POS）----
  // 三原色光谱（高饱和暖调，不刺眼）：蓝(冷·避让·空仓) → 黄(中性) → 朱红(暖·重仓·出击)
  function posColor(c){
    const stops=[
      {p:0,  c:[37,99,235]},  // 蓝  Royal Blue
      {p:50, c:[245,179,1]},  // 黄  金黄（非荧光）
      {p:100,c:[229,72,77]}   // 红  朱红（偏橘，非纯正红）
    ];
    const p=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,c));
    let a=stops[0], b=stops[1];
    if(p>50){ a=stops[1]; b=stops[2]; }
    const t=(p-(p>50?50:0))/50;
    const L=(x,y)=>Math.round(x+(y-x)*t);
    return 'rgb('+L(a.c[0],b.c[0])+','+L(a.c[1],b.c[1])+','+L(a.c[2],b.c[2])+')';
  }
  (function(){
    const el=document.getElementById('posBanner');
    if(!el||!POS) return;
    const c=POS.ceiling||0, cap=POS.cap||66;
    const fill=document.getElementById('posFill');
    const color=posColor(c);
    fill.style.width=c+'%'; fill.style.background=color;
    // 阶段徽章：重度反转红 / 中性黄 / 高潮·持续灰
    const zoneEl=document.getElementById('posZone');
    const ph=POS.phase||'';
    const heavy=(ph==='冰点深化');   // 翻转后：冰点深化(p75 反转甜点)才是高确定性重仓入口；极度反转(弱)降为观望
    const light=(ph==='高潮'||ph==='高潮回落'||ph==='极度冰点持续');
    const pcls=heavy?'strike':(light?'cash':'watch');
    zoneEl.innerHTML='<span class="pos-signal '+pcls+'">'+ph+'</span> <span style="color:#8a90a0;font-size:12px">上限 <b style="color:'+color+'">'+c+'%</b> / 约束 '+cap+'% (基础'+POS.base_ceiling+'%)</span>';
    // hi-low 真数 + 出手频率 + 消息面
    const F=POS.frequency||{}; const fstat=F.status||'';
    const fcol=fstat.indexOf('正常')>=0?'#1a9e5c':(fstat.indexOf('偏少')>=0?'#c47b00':'#c0392b');
    let info='<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.9">';
    info+='hi-low 真数: <b>TC='+POS.tc+'</b> · '+POS.tc_label;
    info+=' ｜ 出手频率: 本月 <b>'+F.month_entries+'</b>次(历史月均 '+F.avg_monthly+') · <span style="color:'+fcol+'">'+fstat+'</span> (约束 '+F.bound[0]+'~'+F.bound[1]+'/月)';
    info+=' ｜ 消息面偏置: <b>'+POS.news_bias+'</b>(可手动调)</div>';
    // 极端指标行（命中 p90 即红标）

    const tag=(on,txt)=>'<span class="extreme-tag '+(on?'on':'off')+'">'+txt+(on?' ⚠':'')+'</span>';
    let ex='<div class="extreme-row">极端指标：'
      +tag(E.pain,'pain '+(POS.pain!=null?(+POS.pain).toFixed(3):'-'))
      +tag(E.dt,'跌停 '+(POS.dt!=null?POS.dt:'-')+' (p90='+(POS.dt_p90!=null?POS.dt_p90:'-')+')')
      +tag(E.fall,'跌超7% '+(POS.fall!=null?POS.fall:'-')+' (p90='+(POS.fall_p90!=null?POS.fall_p90:'-')+')')
      +tag(E.climax,'ztb '+(POS.ztb!=null?POS.ztb:'-')+'% (p90='+(POS.ztb_p90!=null?POS.ztb_p90:'-')+')')
      +'</div>';
    let rv='<div style="margin-top:4px;font-size:12px">冰点反转信号：连日冰点 <b>'+POS.streak+'</b> 天 · 昨日涨停/连板 <b>'+(POS.ztb!=null?POS.ztb:'-')+'% / '+(POS.lb!=null?POS.lb:'-')+'%</b></div>';
    let meta='<b style="color:'+(heavy?'#c0392b':'#555')+'">'+(POS.reason||'')+'</b>'+info+ex+rv
      +'<div style="margin-top:6px;color:var(--sub)">'+(POS.guidance||'')+'</div>'
      +'<div style="margin-top:4px;color:#aab0bb;font-size:11px">动态上限：高潮≤33% · 冰点深化(反转甜点)≤66% · 极度冰点反转≤30% · 常规15~50% · 消息面可手动偏置 · 出手频率约束1.4~12/月</div>';
    document.getElementById('posMeta').innerHTML=meta;
  })();

  async function draw(){
    try{ await ensureChart(); }catch(e){ return; }
    const data=sl(N), L=data.map(d=>d.date);
    const zt=data.map(d=>num(d,'zt_total')), dt=data.map(d=>num(d,'dt_count')), zb=data.map(d=>num(d,'zb_rate'));
    const B=BOARD||{};
    const max10=data.map(d=>{const b=B[d.date];return b?b.max10:(num(d,'b4plus_10cm')?4:num(d,'b3_10cm')?3:num(d,'b2_10cm')?2:num(d,'zt_10cm')?1:0);});
    const max20=data.map(d=>{const b=B[d.date];return b?b.max20:(num(d,'b3plus_20cm')?3:num(d,'b2_20cm')?2:num(d,'zt_20cm')?1:0);});
    const act10=data.map(d=>!!B[d.date]), act20=data.map(d=>!!B[d.date]);
    const fl=data.map(d=>{const v=num(d,'fall_gt7');return v==null?null:Math.max(v,1);});
    if(window._m)window._m.destroy(); if(window._b)window._b.destroy(); if(window._p)window._p.destroy(); if(window._z)window._z.destroy(); if(window._pre)window._pre.destroy();
    window._m=new Chart(cMain,{type:'line',data:{labels:L,datasets:[
      {label:'涨停',data:zt,borderColor:'#d93a3a',backgroundColor:'rgba(217,58,58,.06)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0,yAxisID:'y'},
      {label:'跌停',data:dt,borderColor:'#1a9e5c',backgroundColor:'rgba(26,158,92,.05)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0,yAxisID:'y1'},
      {label:'涨停警戒线(80家)',data:L.map(()=>80),borderColor:'#d93a3a',borderDash:[6,4],borderWidth:1.3,pointRadius:0,fill:false,yAxisID:'y'},
      {label:'跌停警戒线(5家)',data:L.map(()=>5),borderColor:'#1a9e5c',borderDash:[6,4],borderWidth:1.3,pointRadius:0,fill:false,yAxisID:'y1'},
    ]},options:baseOpts({scales:{
      x:{ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{display:false}},
      y:{ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{color:GRID},title:{display:true,text:'涨停',color:'#d93a3a'}},
      y1:{position:'right',ticks:{color:'#1a9e5c',font:{size:10}},grid:{drawOnChartArea:false},title:{display:true,text:'跌停',color:'#1a9e5c'}}
    }})});
    /* 昨日涨停/连板 溢价 */
    if(P && P.length){
      const pd = (N?P.slice(-N):P);
      const pL = pd.map(d=>d.date);
      const pZt = pd.map(d=>d.zt), pLb = pd.map(d=>d.lb);
      const anyThs = pd.some(d=>d.src==='ths');
      const anyCsv  = pd.some(d=>d.src==='csv');
      const anyDb   = pd.some(d=>d.src==='db');
      let srcTip = '数据来源：昨日涨停股池计算';
      if(anyCsv && anyDb) srcTip = '数据来源：历史=情绪CSV / 近期=DB(previous_zt)';
      else if(anyCsv) srcTip = '数据来源：emotion_history.csv';
      else if(anyDb)  srcTip = '数据来源：DB(previous_zt 昨日涨停股池计算)';
      window._pre=new Chart(cPrem,{type:'line',data:{labels:pL,datasets:[
        {label:'昨日涨停溢价%',data:pZt,borderColor:'#d93a3a',backgroundColor:'rgba(217,58,58,.06)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
        {label:'昨日连板溢价%',data:pLb,borderColor:'#3b5bdb',backgroundColor:'rgba(59,91,219,.05)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
        {label:'2%警戒线',data:pL.map(()=>2),borderColor:'#e8850c',borderDash:[6,4],pointRadius:0,borderWidth:1,fill:false},
      {label:'过热风险前瞻线(p90)',data:pL.map(()=> (POS&&POS.ztb_p90!=null?POS.ztb_p90:3.57)),borderColor:'#d93a3a',borderDash:[6,4],pointRadius:0,borderWidth:1,fill:false},
      ]},options:baseOpts({scales:{
        x:{ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{display:false}},
        y:{ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10},callback:v=>v+'%'},grid:{color:GRID}}
      },plugins:{legend:{labels:{boxWidth:12,font:{size:11},color:'#555'}},tooltip:{callbacks:{afterBody:()=> anyThs?'数据来源：同花顺 883900/883958':srcTip}}}})});
    }
    window._z=new Chart(cZb,{type:'line',data:{labels:L,datasets:[
      {label:'炸板率%',data:zb,borderColor:'#e8850c',backgroundColor:'rgba(232,133,12,.08)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
      {label:'炸板率 分歧/洗盘观察区(27%)',data:L.map(()=>27),borderColor:'#e8850c',borderDash:[6,4],borderWidth:1.3,pointRadius:0,fill:false},
    ]},options:baseOpts({scales:{
      x:{ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{display:false}},
      y:{ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10},callback:v=>v+'%'},grid:{color:GRID}}
    }})});
    window._b=new Chart(cBoard,{type:'bar',data:{labels:L,datasets:[
      {label:'10cm最高连板',data:max10,backgroundColor:'rgba(217,58,58,.75)'},
      {label:'20cm最高连板',data:max20,backgroundColor:'rgba(59,91,219,.75)'},
    ]},options:baseOpts({scales:{
      x:{ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{display:false}},
      y:{ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10},stepSize:1,callback:v=>v+'板'},grid:{color:GRID},suggestedMax:5}
    },plugins:{legend:{labels:{boxWidth:12,font:{size:11},color:'#555'}},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>{
      const v=ctx.parsed.y; if(v===0)return ctx.dataset.label+'：无连板';
      const isAct=ctx.datasetIndex===0?act10[ctx.dataIndex]:act20[ctx.dataIndex];
      let suf=v+'板';
      if(!isAct){ suf=(ctx.datasetIndex===0&&v===4)||(ctx.datasetIndex===1&&v===3)?'板+':'板'; suf+='（情绪图估算）'; }
      return ctx.dataset.label+'：'+suf;
    }}}}})});
    window._p=new Chart(cPanic,{type:'line',data:{labels:L,datasets:[
      {label:'跌超7%(对数)',data:fl,borderColor:'#8a90a0',backgroundColor:'rgba(138,144,160,.12)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
    ]},options:baseOpts({scales:{
      x:{ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{display:false}},
      y:{type:'logarithmic',ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{color:GRID}}
    }})});
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#waveTb button').forEach(b=>{
    b.onclick=()=>{document.querySelectorAll('#waveTb button').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));b.classList.add('on');N=+b.dataset.n;draw();};
  });
  window.__registerTab('wave', ()=>draw());
})();

  const tierBox = document.getElementById('tierCards');
  let __ztCurDate = ZT.latest || (ZT.dates && ZT.dates[0]) || null;
  function getZtRows(){ return (ZT.by_date && ZT.by_date[__ztCurDate]) || []; }
  function ztLabel(){ return __ztCurDate ? ('（' + __ztCurDate + '）') : ''; }
  function syncZtDate(){ const o=document.getElementById('ovdate'); if(o)o.textContent=ztLabel(); const z=document.getElementById('ztDate'); if(z)z.textContent=ztLabel(); }
  syncZtDate();

  // 连板数 -> 梯队 key
  function tierKeyOf(s) {
    const lb = s.连板数 || 0;
    if (lb >= 5) return '5plus';
    if (lb === 4) return '4';
    if (lb === 3) return '3';
    if (lb === 2) return '2';
    return '1';
  }
  let activeTier = null;

  function fmtTime(t) {
    if (t == null) return '—';
    t = String(t).padStart(6, '0');
    return t.slice(0, 2) + ':' + t.slice(2, 4) + ':' + t.slice(4, 6);
  }
  function fmtAmt(v) {
    if (v == null) return '—';
    v = Number(v);
    if (v >= 1e8) return (v / 1e8).toFixed(2) + '亿';
    if (v >= 1e4) return (v / 1e4).toFixed(0) + '万';
    return v.toFixed(0);
  }
  function esc(x) {
    return String(x == null ? '' : x).replace(/[&<>"]/g, m => (
      { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[m]));
  }

    { key: '代码', label: '代码', cls: 'grey' },
    { key: '名称', label: '名称' },
    { key: '涨跌幅', label: '涨幅%', fmt: v => { if (v == null) return '—'; const c = v >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green'; return '<span class="' + c + '">' + (v >= 0 ? '+' : '') + v.toFixed(2) + '%</span>'; } },
    { key: '连板数', label: '连板', fmt: v => v != null ? v + '板' : '—' },
    { key: '成交额', label: '成交额', fmt: fmtAmt },

  function renderZtPool() {
    if (!ztBox) return;
    if (!__ztCurDate || getZtRows().length === 0) {
      ztBox.innerHTML = '<div class="note" style="font-size:13px">暂无涨停股池数据。运行 main.py 后会自动填充。</div>';
      return;
    }
    const all = getZtRows();
    let list = activeTier ? all.filter(s => tierKeyOf(s) === activeTier) : all;
    // 10cm / 20cm 筛选
    const cmSel = document.getElementById('ztCmSel');
    const cmVal = cmSel ? cmSel.value : 'all';
    if (cmVal !== 'all') {
      const want20 = cmVal === '20';
      list = list.filter(s => !!s._is20 === want20);
    }
    const sorted = [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      const la = a.连板数 || 0, lb = b.连板数 || 0;
      if (la !== lb) return lb - la;
      return (b.封板资金 || 0) - (a.封板资金 || 0);
    });
    let h = '';
    if (activeTier) {
      h += '<div class="filter-bar">当前筛选：<b>' + tierLab[activeTier] + '</b> <span class="filter-x" onclick="window.__clearTier()">✕ 清除</span></div>';
    }
    h += '<div class="tbl-wrap" style="max-height:480px"><table><thead><tr>' +
      cols.map(c => '<th>' + c.label + '</th>').join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    sorted.forEach(s => {
      const is20 = s._is20;
      const code = s['代码'];
      h += '<tr class="zt-row" data-code="' + code + '" onclick="window.__toggleReason(\'' + code + '\')">' + cols.map(c => {
        let val = s[c.key];
        let cell = c.fmt ? c.fmt(val) : (val == null ? '—' : val);
        let cls = c.cls || '';
        if (c.key === '连板数' && val >= 3) cls = 'red';
        if (c.key === '炸板次数' && val > 5) cls = 'orange';
        if (c.key === '名称' && is20) cell += ' <span class="tag ok">20cm</span>';
        return '<td class="' + cls + '">' + cell + '</td>';
      }).join('') + '</tr>';
      // 紧跟该行的隐藏展开行（点击时 toggle 显示）
      const rb = s['涨停原因_简略'] || '', rd = s['涨停原因_详细'] || '', rs = s['原因来源'] || '';
      const hasR = rb || rd || rs;
      h += '<tr class="reason-expand" id="rz-' + code + '"><td colspan="' + cols.length + '"><div class="rz-inner">' +
        (hasR
          ? (rb ? '<div class="rz-reason">涨停原因：' + esc(rb) + '</div>' : '') +
            (rd ? '<div class="rz-detail">' + esc(rd) + '</div>' : '') +
            (rs ? '<div class="rz-src">来源：' + esc(rs) + '</div>' : '')
          : '<div class="rz-empty">暂无涨停原因数据</div>') +
        '</div></td></tr>';
    });
    h += '</tbody></table></div>';
    h += '<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:var(--sub)">共 <b>' + sorted.length + '</b> / ' + all.length + ' 只涨停股' +
      (activeTier ? '（已按梯队筛选）' : '') +
      (cmVal !== 'all' ? '（已按' + (cmVal === '20' ? '20cm' : '10cm') + '筛选）' : '') + ' | 按「连板数↓ 封板资金↓」排序</div>';
    ztBox.innerHTML = h;
  }
  // ---- 手风琴：点击个股行 → 紧跟该行展开/收起涨停原因 ----
  window.__toggleReason = function (code) {
    const el = document.getElementById('rz-' + code);
    if (!el) return;
    const willShow = !el.classList.contains('show');
    // 先收起所有其他展开行（手风琴：同时只展开一个）
    document.querySelectorAll('.reason-expand.show').forEach(e => { if (e !== el) e.classList.remove('show'); });
    el.classList.toggle('show', willShow);
  };
  window.__clearTier = function () {
    activeTier = null;
    document.querySelectorAll('#tierCards .tier').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on'));
    renderZtPool();
  };

  // 梯队卡片（点击 -> 筛选下方明细），按当前日期重绘
  function renderTierCards() {
    if (!tierBox) return;
    tierBox.innerHTML = '';
    const tiers = [{ k: '5plus', lab: '5 板以上', goal: '明日冲 6 板' }, { k: '4', lab: '4 板', goal: '明日冲 5 板' }, { k: '3', lab: '3 板', goal: '明日冲 4 板' }, { k: '2', lab: '2 板', goal: '明日冲 3 板' }, { k: '1', lab: '首板', goal: '明日冲 2 板' }];
    const cnt = {}; tiers.forEach(t => cnt[t.k] = 0);
    getZtRows().forEach(s => { cnt[tierKeyOf(s)]++; });
    tiers.forEach(t => {
      const div = document.createElement('div'); div.className = 'tier'; div.dataset.tier = t.k;
      div.innerHTML = `<div class="lab">${t.lab}</div><div class="cnt">${cnt[t.k]}</div><div class="goal">${t.goal}</div>`;
      div.onclick = () => {
        const was = div.classList.contains('on');
        document.querySelectorAll('#tierCards .tier').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on'));
        if (!was) { div.classList.add('on'); activeTier = t.k; } else { activeTier = null; }
        renderZtPool();
      };
      tierBox.appendChild(div);
    });
  }

  // ---- 日期筛选器（涨停池）----
  const ztDateSel = document.getElementById('ztDateSel');
  if (ztDateSel && ZT.dates && ZT.dates.length) {
    ZT.dates.forEach(d => {
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = d; opt.textContent = d;
      if (d === __ztCurDate) opt.selected = true;
      ztDateSel.appendChild(opt);
    });
    ztDateSel.onchange = () => {
      __ztCurDate = ztDateSel.value;
      activeTier = null;
      document.querySelectorAll('#tierCards .tier').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on'));
      syncZtDate();
      renderTierCards();
      renderZtPool();
    };
  }
  // ---- 10cm / 20cm 筛选器（涨停池，紧随日期）----
  const ztCmSel = document.getElementById('ztCmSel');
  if (ztCmSel) {
    ztCmSel.onchange = () => { renderZtPool(); };
  }

  renderTierCards();
  renderZtPool();

  const agg=DATA.agg_history||[];
  const rp=v=>v==null?null:v*100;
  let chart;
  async function drawAgg(range){
    try{ await ensureChart(); }catch(e){ return; }
    const data=agg.slice(-range),L=data.map(d=>d.date);
    const ds=[
      {label:'1进2',data:data.map(d=>rp(d.r_1to2)),borderColor:'#d93a3a',backgroundColor:'rgba(217,58,58,.08)',fill:true,tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
      {label:'2进3',data:data.map(d=>rp(d.r_2to3)),borderColor:'#e8850c',tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
      {label:'3进4',data:data.map(d=>rp(d.r_3to4)),borderColor:'#3b5bdb',tension:.3,pointRadius:0},
    ];
    if(chart)chart.destroy();
    chart=new Chart(aggChart,{type:'line',data:{labels:L,datasets:ds},options:baseOpts({scales:{
      x:{ticks:{maxTicksLimit:12,color:TICK,font:{size:10}},grid:{display:false}},
      y:{ticks:{color:TICK,font:{size:10},callback:v=>v+'%'},grid:{color:GRID}}
    }})});
    const last=data[data.length-1];
    document.getElementById('aggNote').innerHTML=`最新（${last.date}）数量口径：1进2 <b class="red">${last.r_1to2==null?'—':(last.r_1to2*100).toFixed(1)+'%'}</b> ｜ 2进3 <b class="orange">${last.r_2to3==null?'—':(last.r_2to3*100).toFixed(1)+'%'}</b> ｜ 3进4 <b class="blue">${last.r_3to4==null?'—':(last.r_3to4*100).toFixed(1)+'%'}</b><br>4进5/5板以上因历史仅存4板+聚合数，数量口径无法拆分，需个股精确口径（≥2交易日）。`;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#lbTb button').forEach(b=>{b.onclick=()=>{document.querySelectorAll('#lbTb button').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));b.classList.add('on');drawAgg(+b.dataset.r);};});
  window.__registerTab('lb', ()=>drawAgg(9999));
  const detail=DATA.stock_detail||[],box=document.getElementById('detailBox');
  if(detail.length===0){
    box.innerHTML=`<div class="note" style="font-size:13px;color:#2a2d36;line-height:1.9"><b>个股级晋级率暂无可计算数据。</b><br>精确晋级率需连续两个交易日的涨停股池+次日涨跌幅。当前 stock_pools.db 仅 ${DATA.latest_pool_date||'—'} 共 1 个交易日。<br>此后<b>每日运行 main.py</b> 累积到≥2天，重算即可自动填充：1进2/2进3/3进4/4进5/5板以上 的成功个股、失败个股及次日涨幅。<br>当前可用：上方「连板梯队总览」即最新一天明日晋级观察池。</div>`;
  }else{
    const sel=document.createElement('select');
    // 降序：最新晋级区间在前、默认选中
    const dIdx=detail.map((_,i)=>i).sort((a,b)=>(detail[b].entry_date).localeCompare(detail[a].entry_date));
    dIdx.forEach(i=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=i;o.textContent=detail[i].entry_date+' → '+detail[i].next_date;sel.appendChild(o);});
    const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.style.marginBottom='12px';wrap.appendChild(sel);box.appendChild(wrap);
    const content=document.createElement('div');box.appendChild(content);
    function render(i){const d=detail[i];let h='';Object.values(d.levels).forEach(L=>{
      const rate=L.rate==null?'—':(L.rate*100).toFixed(1)+'%';
      h+=`<div class="lvl"><div class="hd"><span class="nm">${L.label}</span><span class="rt ${L.rate>=0.5?'red':'orange'}">${rate}</span><span class="meta">晋级 ${L.success} / 候选 ${L.total}（失败 ${L.fail}）</span></div>`;
      if(L.success_stocks.length)h+=`<div style="margin:6px 0"><b class="red">晋级成功（${L.success_stocks.length}）</b><table><tr><th>名称</th><th>代码</th><th>晋级</th></tr>${L.success_stocks.map(s=>`<tr><td>${s.名称}</td><td class="grey">${s.代码}</td><td><span class="tag ok">${lb(s.t_lianban)}→${lb(s.t1_lianban)}</span></td></tr>`).join('')}</table></div>`;
      if(L.fail_stocks.length)h+=`<div style="margin:6px 0"><b class="green">晋级失败（${L.fail_stocks.length}）</b><table><tr><th>名称</th><th>代码</th><th>原连板</th><th>次日涨幅</th></tr>${L.fail_stocks.map(s=>`<tr><td>${s.名称}</td><td class="grey">${s.代码}</td><td>${lb(s.t_lianban)}</td><td class="${s.next_chg!=null?(s.next_chg>=0?'red':'green'):''}">${fmt(s.next_chg)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table></div>`;
      h+=`</div>`;});content.innerHTML=h;}
    sel.onchange=()=>render(+sel.value);render(dIdx[0]);
  }
})();

/* ================= 数据库表浏览器（sql.js，数据库内联免上传） ================= */
(function(){
  let SQL=null, db=null, curTable=null, page=0, pageSize=200, total=0;
  const fileInput=document.getElementById('dbFile');
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('dbStatus');
  const b64El=document.getElementById('dbB64');
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#dbTable tbody');
  const thead=document.querySelector('#dbTable thead');
  const prevBtn=document.getElementById('prevPage'), nextBtn=document.getElementById('nextPage'), pageInfo=document.getElementById('pageInfo');
  function setStatus(t){statusEl.innerHTML=t;}
  async function ensureSQL(){
    if(SQL)return SQL;
    if(typeof initSqlJs==='undefined'){setStatus('⚠ 未能加载 SQLite 引擎（sql.js），请检查网络后刷新。');throw new Error('no sql.js');}
    setStatus('加载 SQLite 引擎（首次需联网，之后浏览器缓存）…');
    SQL=await initSqlJs({locateFile:f=>'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/sql.js/1.10.3/'+f});
    return SQL;
  }
  function loadFromBytes(uint8, name){
    try{db=new SQL.Database(uint8);}catch(e){setStatus('打开失败：'+e.message);return false;}
    let tcount=0;try{tcount=db.exec("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")[0].values[0][0];}catch(e){}
    setStatus('已载入 <b>'+name+'</b>，点击上方表名查看（共 '+tcount+' 张表）');
    return true;
  }
  async function autoLoad(){
    let sql;
    try{ sql=await ensureSQL(); }catch(e){ setStatus('⚠ 未能加载 SQLite 引擎，请检查网络后刷新，或用「重新选择本地库」上传。'); return; }
    SQL=sql;
    const b64=(b64El&&b64El.value||'').trim();
    if(!b64){ setStatus('未内联数据库，请点「⟳ 重新选择本地库」上传 stock_pools.db。'); return; }
    try{
      const bin=atob(b64);
      const bytes=new Uint8Array(bin.length);
      for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++) bytes[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
      loadFromBytes(bytes, 'stock_pools.db（内联）');
      if(curTable) query(curTable,0);
    }catch(e){ setStatus('内联数据库解析失败：'+e.message+'，可点「⟳ 重新选择本地库」重新上传。'); }
  }
  async function onFile(file){
    try{await ensureSQL();}catch(e){return;}
    setStatus('读取数据库：'+file.name);
    const buf=await file.arrayBuffer();
    if(loadFromBytes(new Uint8Array(buf), file.name) && curTable) query(curTable,0);
  }
  function query(table,p){
    if(!db){fileInput.click();curTable=table;return;}
    curTable=table;page=p;
    try{
      const c=db.exec('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM "'+table+'"')[0].values[0][0];total=c;
      const res=db.exec('SELECT * FROM "'+table+'" LIMIT '+pageSize+' OFFSET '+(p*pageSize));
      if(!res.length){thead.innerHTML='';tbody.innerHTML='';setStatus('表 '+table+' 为空');return;}
      render(table,res[0].columns,res[0].values);
      setStatus('表 <b>'+table+'</b> ｜ 共 '+total+' 行，第 '+(p+1)+' 页');
      prevBtn.disabled=p<=0; nextBtn.disabled=(p+1)*pageSize>=total;
      pageInfo.textContent='第 '+(p+1)+' / '+Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/pageSize))+' 页';
    }catch(e){setStatus('查询失败：'+e.message);}
  }
  function render(table,cols,vals){
    thead.innerHTML='<tr>'+cols.map(c=>'<th>'+c+'</th>').join('')+'</tr>';
    tbody.innerHTML=vals.map(r=>'<tr>'+r.map((v,i)=>{
      let cls='';const cn=cols[i];
      if((cn==='涨跌幅'||cn==='次日涨跌幅'||cn==='next_chg')&&typeof v==='number')cls=v>=0?'red':'green';
      if(cn==='代码'||cn==='名称')cls='grey';
      return '<td class="'+cls+'">'+(v==null?'':v)+'</td>';
    }).join('')+'</tr>').join('');
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('.db-bar button').forEach(b=>{
    if(b.id==='dbReload')return;  // 该按钮单独绑定，不触发表查询
    b.onclick=()=>{ if(!db){fileInput.click();curTable=b.dataset.t;} else query(b.dataset.t,0); };
  });
  const reBtn=document.getElementById('dbReload');
  if(reBtn) reBtn.onclick=()=>fileInput.click();
  fileInput.onchange=e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(f)onFile(f);};
  prevBtn.onclick=()=>{if(curTable&&page>0)query(curTable,page-1);};
  nextBtn.onclick=()=>{if(curTable)query(curTable,page+1);};
  // 改为「点开「数据库表」tab 时才加载 SQLite 引擎」：主页面打开更快、可离线
  let __dbLoaded=false;
  const __dbTabBtn=document.querySelector('nav button[data-tab="db"]');
  if(__dbTabBtn)__dbTabBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{ if(!__dbLoaded){__dbLoaded=true; autoLoad();} });
})();

"净额": -10.24}, {"序号": 88, "行业": "元件", "公司家数": 62, "行业指数": 18688.4, "阶段涨跌幅": "-37.72%", "流入资金": 577.39, "流出资金": 645.99, "净额": -68.6}, {"序号": 89, "行业": "半导体", "公司家数": 183, "行业指数": 13932.1, "阶段涨跌幅": "-38.69%", "流入资金": 2449.54, "流出资金": 2493.76, "净额": -44.21}, {"序号": 90, "行业": "电子化学品", "公司家数": 43, "行业指数": 72692.1, "阶段涨跌幅": "-45.06%", "流入资金": 380.54, "流出资金": 389.2, "净额": -8.66}]}};
  const box = document.getElementById('sectorBox');
  const chartBox = document.getElementById('sectorChart');
  if (!box) return;
  if (S.error || !S.data || Object.keys(S.data).length === 0) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="note" style="font-size:13px">暂无行业资金流数据：' + (S.error || 'akshare 抓取失败') + '。请确认 akshare 已安装且网络可达。</div>';
    return;
  }
  // 数值化：akshare 排行口径返回的是字符串（如 "15.77%"），需剥离 % / 逗号 / 单位后再转数
  function num(v) {
    if (v == null) return 0;
    if (typeof v === 'string') v = v.replace(/[%,\s亿万元]/g, '');
    const n = Number(v);
    return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n;
  }
  function isNil(v) { return v == null || v === '' || v === '-' || v === '--'; }
  let curSym = "即时";
  // 键名兼容：按钮 data-sym 是「3日排行」，后端 S.data 键是「3日」——两种写法都能取到
  function getRows() { const d = S.data[curSym] || S.data[String(curSym).replace('排行','')] || S.data[String(curSym)+'排行']; return Array.isArray(d) ? d : []; }

  // 净额降序排名（用于「热」标签，仅当前口径内）
  let netRank = {};
  function rebuildRank() {
    netRank = {};
    [...getRows()].sort((a, b) => num(b['净额']) - num(a['净额'])).forEach((s, i) => { netRank[s['行业']] = i; });
  }

  // 动态列：即时含领涨股/领涨涨幅/现价；排行不含
  function buildCols(rows) {
    // 涨幅列自适应：akshare 各口径列名不同（涨跌幅 / 阶段涨跌幅 / 行业-涨跌幅），按优先级探测
    const r0 = rows.length ? rows[0] : {};
    const pctKey = ['涨跌幅', '阶段涨跌幅', '行业-涨跌幅'].find(k => k in r0) || '涨跌幅';
    const pctLabel = (pctKey === '阶段涨跌幅') ? '阶段涨幅%' : '涨幅%';
    const cols = [
      { key: '序号', label: '#' },
      { key: '行业', label: '行业' },
      { key: pctKey, label: pctLabel, fmt: v => { if (isNil(v)) return '—'; const n = num(v); const c = n >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green'; return '<span class="' + c + '">' + (n >= 0 ? '+' : '') + n.toFixed(2) + '%</span>'; } },
      { key: '流入资金', label: '流入(亿)', fmt: v => isNil(v) ? '—' : num(v).toFixed(2) },
      { key: '流出资金', label: '流出(亿)', fmt: v => isNil(v) ? '—' : num(v).toFixed(2) },
      { key: '净额', label: '净额(亿)', fmt: v => { if (isNil(v)) return '—'; const n = num(v); const c = n >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green'; return '<span class="' + c + '">' + (n >= 0 ? '+' : '') + n.toFixed(2) + '</span>'; } },
      { key: '公司家数', label: '家数' },
    ];
    if (rows.length && '领涨股' in rows[0]) {
      cols.push({ key: '领涨股', label: '领涨股' });
      cols.push({ key: '领涨股-涨跌幅', label: '领涨涨幅', fmt: v => { if (isNil(v)) return '—'; const n = num(v); const c = n >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green'; return '<span class="' + c + '">' + (n >= 0 ? '+' : '') + n.toFixed(2) + '%</span>'; } });
      if ('当前价' in rows[0]) cols.push({ key: '当前价', label: '现价', cls: 'grey' });
    }
    return cols;
  }

  let sortKey = '净额', sortDir = 'desc';
  let lastCols = [];
  function colLabel(k) { const c = lastCols.find(c => c.key === k); return c ? c.label : k; }
  function sortedRows() {
    const arr = [...getRows()];
    const isStr = (sortKey === '行业' || sortKey === '领涨股');
    arr.sort((a, b) => {
      let va = a[sortKey], vb = b[sortKey];
      if (isStr) { va = va == null ? '' : String(va); vb = vb == null ? '' : String(vb); return sortDir === 'asc' ? va.localeCompare(vb, 'zh') : vb.localeCompare(va, 'zh'); }
      return sortDir === 'asc' ? num(va) - num(vb) : num(vb) - num(va);
    });
    return arr;
  }

  // 分布条形图：当前排序下 Top20 + 末20（数值列），红涨绿跌
  let chart;
  async function renderChart() {
    try{ await ensureChart(); }catch(e){ return; }
    if (!chartBox) return;
    const full = sortedRows();
    const isStr = (sortKey === '行业' || sortKey === '领涨股');
    const set = isStr ? full.slice(0, 40) : full.slice(0, 20).concat(full.slice(-20));
    const labels = set.map(d => d['行业']);
    const vals = set.map(d => num(d[isStr ? '净额' : sortKey]));
    const colors = vals.map(v => v >= 0 ? '#d93a3a' : '#1a9e5c');
    if (chart) chart.destroy();
    chart = new Chart(chartBox, {
      type: 'bar',
      data: { labels, datasets: [{ label: (isStr ? '净额(亿)' : (sortKey + '(亿)')), data: vals, backgroundColor: colors }] },
      options: baseOpts({
        indexAxis: 'y', responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => (ctx.parsed.x >= 0 ? '+' : '') + ctx.parsed.x.toFixed(2) } } },
        scales: { x: { ticks: { color: TICK, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: GRID } }, y: { ticks: { color: '#555', font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { display: false } } }
      })
    });
  }

  function renderTable() {
    const rows = getRows();
    const cols = buildCols(rows); lastCols = cols;
    const sortableKeys = new Set(cols.map(c => c.key));
    const data = sortedRows();
    let h = '<div class="tbl-wrap" style="max-height:560px"><table><thead><tr>' +
      cols.map(c => {
        let arr = '';
        if (sortableKeys.has(c.key)) {
          if (c.key === sortKey) arr = ' <span class="arr">' + (sortDir === 'asc' ? '▲' : '▼') + '</span>';
          else arr = ' <span class="arr" style="color:var(--sub)">⇅</span>';
        }
        const cls = sortableKeys.has(c.key) ? 'sortable' : '';
        return '<th class="' + cls + '" data-k="' + c.key + '">' + c.label + arr + '</th>';
      }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    data.forEach(s => {
      h += '<tr>' + cols.map(c => {
        let val = s[c.key];
        let cell = c.fmt ? c.fmt(val, s) : (val == null ? '—' : val);
        let cls = '';
        if (c.key === '净额' || c.key === '涨跌幅' || c.key === '阶段涨跌幅' || c.key === '行业-涨跌幅' || c.key === '领涨股-涨跌幅') cls = num(val) >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green';
        if (c.key === '行业' && num(s['净额']) > 0 && netRank[s['行业']] < 3) cell += ' <span class="tag ok">热</span>';
        return '<td class="' + cls + '">' + cell + '</td>';
      }).join('') + '</tr>';
    });
    h += '</tbody></table></div>';
    const dirTxt = sortDir === 'asc' ? '升序' : '降序';
    h += '<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:var(--sub)">口径：<b>' + curSym + '</b> ｜ 共 <b>' + data.length + '</b> 个行业 | 点击表头可切换 升/降序 | 当前排序：<b>' + colLabel(sortKey) + '</b> ' + dirTxt + '</div>';
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
      th.onclick = () => {
        const k = th.dataset.k;
        if (k === sortKey) sortDir = sortDir === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
        else { sortKey = k; sortDir = (k === '行业' || k === '领涨股') ? 'asc' : 'desc'; }
        renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb();
      };
    });
  }

  // 排序工具栏
  const tb = document.getElementById('sectorTb');
  function syncTb() {
    if (!tb) return;
    tb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.k === sortKey && b.dataset.dir === sortDir); });
  }
  if (tb) {
    tb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
      b.onclick = () => { sortKey = b.dataset.k; sortDir = b.dataset.dir; renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb(); };
    });
  }

  // 口径筛选（即时 / 3日 / 5日 / 10日 / 20日）
  const symTb = document.getElementById('sectorSymTb');
  function syncSymTb() {
    if (!symTb) return;
    symTb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.sym === curSym); });
  }
  if (symTb) {
    symTb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
      b.onclick = () => {
        curSym = b.dataset.sym;
        syncSymTb();   // 先高亮，避免后续异常导致按钮看起来「点了没反应」
        try {
          // 切换口径后，该口径可能不含当前排序列（如排行无领涨股），回退到净额
          const cols = buildCols(getRows());
          if (!cols.some(c => c.key === sortKey)) { sortKey = '净额'; sortDir = 'desc'; }
          rebuildRank();
          renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb();
        } catch (err) {
          box.innerHTML = '<div class="note" style="font-size:13px;color:#e5484d">渲染「' + curSym + '」失败：' + (err && err.message ? err.message : err) + '</div>';
          console.error('[sector] render failed', err);
        }
      };
    });
  }

  window.__registerTab('sector', ()=>{ rebuildRank(); renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb(); syncSymTb(); });
})();

/* ================= 概念板块指数 ================= */
(function(){

  const box = document.getElementById('conceptBox');
  const chartBox = document.getElementById('conceptChart');
  if (!box) return;
  if (C.error || !C.dates || C.dates.length === 0) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="note" style="font-size:13px">暂无概念板块数据：' + (C.error || '请先运行 fetch_concept_board.py 抓取数据') + '。</div>';
    return;
  }
  // 多日数据：默认显示 latest，可通过日期筛选器切换
  let __curDate = C.latest || C.dates[0];
  function getRows() { return C.by_date[__curDate] || []; }
  const rows = getRows();
  function num(v) { if (v == null) return 0; const n = Number(v); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; }

  const cols = [
    { key: '概念名称', label: '概念' },
    { key: '概念代码', label: '代码', cls: 'grey' },
    { key: '板块涨幅', label: '涨幅%', fmt: v => { if (v == null) return '—'; const c = v >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green'; return '<span class="' + c + '">' + (v >= 0 ? '+' : '') + v.toFixed(2) + '%</span>'; } },
    { key: '涨家数', label: '涨' },
    { key: '跌家数', label: '跌' },
    { key: '资金净流入_亿', label: '净流入(亿)', fmt: v => { if (v == null) return '—'; const c = v >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green'; return '<span class="' + c + '">' + (v >= 0 ? '+' : '') + v.toFixed(2) + '</span>'; } },
    { key: '成交额_亿', label: '成交额(亿)', fmt: v => v != null ? v.toFixed(1) : '—' },
    { key: '今开', label: '今开', cls: 'grey' },
    { key: '昨收', label: '昨收', cls: 'grey' },
    { key: '最高', label: '最高', cls: 'grey' },
    { key: '最低', label: '最低', cls: 'grey' },
    { key: '涨幅排名', label: '排名', cls: 'grey' },
  ];
  const sortableKeys = new Set(cols.map(c => c.key));
  let sortKey = '板块涨幅', sortDir = 'desc';

  function sortedRows() {
    const arr = [...getRows()];
    arr.sort((a, b) => {
      let va = a[sortKey], vb = b[sortKey];
      va = num(va); vb = num(vb);
      return sortDir === 'asc' ? va - vb : vb - va;
    });
    return arr;
  }

  // 分布条形图：当前排序下 Top20 + Bottom20
  let chart;
  async function renderChart() {
    try{ await ensureChart(); }catch(e){ return; }
    if (!chartBox) return;
    const full = sortedRows();
    let set = full.slice(0, 20).concat(full.slice(-20));
    const labels = set.map(d => d['概念名称']);
    const vals = set.map(d => num(d['板块涨幅']));
    const colors = vals.map(v => v >= 0 ? '#d93a3a' : '#1a9e5c');
    if (chart) chart.destroy();
    chart = new Chart(chartBox, {
      type: 'bar',
      data: { labels, datasets: [{ label: '板块涨幅%', data: vals, backgroundColor: colors }] },
      options: baseOpts({
        indexAxis: 'y', responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => (ctx.parsed.x >= 0 ? '+' : '') + ctx.parsed.x.toFixed(2) + '%' } } },
        scales: {
          x: { ticks: { color: TICK, font: { size: 10 }, callback: v => v + '%' }, grid: { color: GRID } },
          y: { ticks: { color: '#555', font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { display: false } }
        }
      })
    });
  }

  function renderTable() {
    const data = sortedRows();
    let h = '<div class="tbl-wrap" style="max-height:560px"><table><thead><tr>' +
      cols.map(c => {
        let arr = '';
        if (sortableKeys.has(c.key)) {
          if (c.key === sortKey) arr = ' <span class="arr">' + (sortDir === 'asc' ? '▲' : '▼') + '</span>';
          else arr = ' <span class="arr" style="color:var(--sub)">⇅</span>';
        }
        const cls = sortableKeys.has(c.key) ? 'sortable' : '';
        return '<th class="' + cls + '" data-k="' + c.key + '">' + c.label + arr + '</th>';
      }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    data.forEach(s => {
      h += '<tr>' + cols.map(c => {
        let val = s[c.key];
        let cell = c.fmt ? c.fmt(val) : (val == null ? '—' : val);
        let cls = '';
        if (c.key === '板块涨幅') cls = val >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green';
        if (c.key === '资金净流入_亿') cls = val >= 0 ? 'red' : 'green';
        // 涨幅前3名高亮
        if (c.key === '概念名称' && s['板块涨幅'] != null && s['板块涨幅'] > 0) {
          const rankIdx = data.indexOf(s);
          if (rankIdx < 3) cell += ' <span class="tag ok">热</span>';
        }
        return '<td class="' + cls + '">' + cell + '</td>';
      }).join('') + '</tr>';
    });
    h += '</tbody></table></div>';
    const dirTxt = sortDir === 'asc' ? '升序' : '降序';
    h += '<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:var(--sub)">共 <b>' + data.length + '</b> 个概念 | 点击表头可切换 升/降序 | 当前排序：<b>' + colLabel(sortKey) + '</b> ' + dirTxt + ' | 数据日期：<b>' + (C.date || '—') + '</b></div>';
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
      th.onclick = () => {
        const k = th.dataset.k;
        if (k === sortKey) sortDir = sortDir === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
        else { sortKey = k; sortDir = 'desc'; }
        renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb();
      };
    });
  }
  function colLabel(k) { const c = cols.find(c => c.key === k); return c ? c.label : k; }

  const tb = document.getElementById('conceptTb');
  function syncTb() {
    if (!tb) return;
    tb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
      b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.k === sortKey && b.dataset.dir === sortDir);
    });
  }
  if (tb) {
    tb.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
      b.onclick = () => { sortKey = b.dataset.k; sortDir = b.dataset.dir; renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb(); };
    });
  }
  // ---- 日期筛选器 ----
  const dateSel = document.getElementById('conceptDateSel');
  if (dateSel && C.dates) {
    C.dates.forEach(d => {
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = d; opt.textContent = d;
      if (d === __curDate) opt.selected = true;
      dateSel.appendChild(opt);
    });
    dateSel.onchange = () => {
      __curDate = dateSel.value;
      renderTable(); renderChart();
    };
  }

  window.__registerTab('concept', ()=>{ renderTable(); renderChart(); syncTb(); });
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
